## Miro-style features phase (milestones 2, 4, 5 scope): built and integrated (2026-09-27)

Per-feature checklist coverage: docs/FEATURES-M2-CONTRACTS.md §3 "As built".

- **Canvas UX**: Miro shortcuts (L connector, P pen, Alt+1/2, Ctrl+0, Ctrl+Shift+L, PgUp/PgDn, M minimap, Ctrl+F search), guides on by default, type-to-edit notes, paste-as-sticky-notes prompt, Draftroom 13-colour WCAG-AA note palette, Miro-style toolbar, template picker/insert (one undo), frame outline with reorder, board search, copy link to selection, start view, 20k shape cap.
- **Comments**: comment tool (C), shape-anchored pins that move with shapes, threads with replies/edit/delete, resolve/reopen, read/unread/mentioned states, @mentions (in-app notifications; no email), comments panel with filters, notifications bell, orphaned threads, Shift+C.
- **History**: named checkpoints, restore as new private board, duplicate, board JSON export/import, board-wide "recently deleted" with restore (captured server-side), periodic consistent SQLite backups + restore drill.
- **Present & export**: frame-by-frame presentation (hides chrome/cursors/pins), PNG/SVG/PDF (raster pages + text layer)/CSV export.
- **Workshops**: server-timed timer, voting with transactional budgets and ballots hidden until reveal, results, "gather everyone" (follow).
- **Dashboard & sharing**: starred, grid/list, template gallery on New board, duplicate/move/import, sharing-state badge, review links (multi-use, expiring), access requests with owner approval, pending-invitation list.
- **Platform**: production refuses loopback internal URLs, TRUST_PROXY, sync Origin allowlist, separate sync origin for Render, render.yaml/Docker updated.

Checks: pnpm verify — lint 0 errors / 0 warnings, typecheck, vitest 1245 passed / 1 skipped, Playwright 14/14 (M0/M1 suites; feature e2e suites are the next phase); integration smoke 53/53 in branded Chrome with four users.

## Validation phase (feature e2e + collaboration quality): COMPLETE locally (2026-09-27)

Seven engineers proved the features in real browsers (two or three contexts, fresh contexts and the API as the second witness) and fixed what failed; an integration pass ran everything together. Per-ID status with the e2e test names: docs/FEATURES-M2-CONTRACTS.md §3; scenario map: docs/E2E-PLAN.md (66/66 covered, 4 with a stated deviation).

- **Collaboration quality**: truthful save indicator (Saved / Saving… / Offline / Offline — changes will sync / Change not saved / View only) and per-property undo (WRK-03, WRK-05), plus a guard for a tldraw rebase that wiped the redo stack.
- **Bugs found and fixed**: viewer/commenter user-record push loop; "Saved" while an image was uploading; event stream dead after an api restart (proxy + data ping + watchdog); edits lost when leaving a board within a second (leave guard); ballots leaking through selection presence; workshop timer offset; no new vote after "End without results"; PNG exports with two pHYs chunks; restore summary counting binding ends; comment Esc/focus; share badge and Share dialog not live for M1 changes; import title; start view not observable; right-click "Unlock all" missing.
- **One-command dev flow** re-checked from an empty `.data`: `pnpm dev` → sign in → New board → a note placed with N shows Saving… then Saved → a second browser loads it; one event stream per board under StrictMode; stopping the tree left nothing listening.

Checks: pnpm verify — lint 0 errors / 0 warnings (504 files), typecheck 13 projects, vitest 1298 passed / 1 skipped (POSIX-only SIGINT test), Playwright 113/113 in 7.7 min; the full browser suite also passed 3 consecutive runs before that (113/113 each).

## Log

### 2026-09-26
- Repository created; plan documents copied to `docs/`. Repo rules in `AGENTS.md` (read by Cursor and, via `CLAUDE.md`, by Claude Code).
- Toolchain: Node 24.19.0 LTS (winget), pnpm 10.34.5, git 2.55, ffmpeg 9.0.1. Python not installed.
- tldraw / @tldraw/sync / @tldraw/sync-core latest = **5.4.2** — newer than model training data, so every API was verified from the published packages (six research reports + a critic synthesis in `docs/research/`).
- Hazards found in the research and fixed in our wiring (all in the official example pattern):
  1. `SQLiteSyncStorage`'s throttled tombstone prune crashes the process if the DB closes within 1 s of a delete → `pruneTombstones.flush()` before `db.close()`.
  2. Passing the raw `ws` to `handleSocketConnect` lets a stale socket's late `close` kill the reconnected session → wrapper without `addEventListener` + current-socket guard.
  3. Mixed-case board ids open two rooms on one SQLite file on case-insensitive filesystems (Windows/macOS) → lowercase-only ids.
  4. `new SQLiteSyncStorage({ snapshot })` on an existing DB wipes it → never pass `snapshot` to an initialized DB.
- Durability facts: each push commits in one synchronous SQLite transaction before `push_result`/broadcast; the library sets no pragmas, so we set WAL + `synchronous=FULL` + `busy_timeout`.
- Conflict semantics (documented, SYNTHESIS §8): per-prop last-writer-wins; same-note typing replaces the whole rich-text doc (characters lost when edits cross) → server-enforced text lease; delete beats edit; undo writes back the whole earlier record (can revert others' props / resurrect deleted shapes).
- License: dev on localhost is free (watermark). Any HTTPS production host without a domain-matching key blanks the editor after 5 s; price is quote-only (open question for tldraw sales; see `docs/research/license.md` §10).
- Workspace scaffolded from the verified skeleton; baseline two-browser e2e (edits, presence, reload, SIGKILL/restart, offline reconnect) passing through the same-origin Vite proxy.
- Milestone-0 build started with parallel owners (sync, web, worker, protocol tests) against `docs/M0-CONTRACTS.md`, followed by an integration/e2e pass.
- M0 build: sync file routes + text lease, preview worker, protocol test harness, branded web client, and browser e2e suites landed; e2e lease-test flake root-caused (caret placement on an already-selected note) and fixed.
- Integration fixes: worker file-type moved to runtime deps; web declares @draftroom/dev-assets; asset id validation aligned with the canvas-schema (10–40 chars); apps/sync typecheck now covers test/.
- M1 dependencies pinned and installed (Fastify 5.12.5, PGlite 0.5.8 = PostgreSQL 18.3 in-process, pg 8.23.0, zod 4.6.5, @clerk/backend 3.20.1, @clerk/react 6.17.2); PGlite smoke-tested (memory + file persistence).
- docs/FEASIBILITY.md, docs/DEMO.md written. Milestone 0 complete.
- Board logic packages: board-ops (bulk notes, search, frame outline/presentation order, CSV, archive import/export, id remap) and six templates; 372 tests; adversarial review fixed 7 defects.
- Miro UX research: official behaviors, user voices, and a 96-item P0 checklist (docs/research/miro-*.md).
- Milestone 1 built and integrated (api, db, auth, permissions, tickets, revocation, dashboard, share dialog, invitations, deploy config). Integration fixes: apps/sync and apps/web now declare @draftroom/permissions.

- Deployed the rename and the security fixes (0e58e2e) to staging: api → sync → web all went live on the first try, and the live sign-in page reads "DBC Lobby". With the owner's go-ahead, deleted the unused 5 GB api disk and raised the per-workspace file storage to 100 GB (`DRAFTROOM_WORKSPACE_QUOTA_MB`, render.yaml). A Blueprint env change redeploys the api by itself.
- Measured on staging: requests through the static-site rewrite and straight to the api land in the same per-IP rate-limit bucket (DEPLOY.md "Client IPs"), so per-IP limits key on the real client.
- **Wave 3** (docs/WAVE3-CONTRACTS.md), built by six owners, with an adversarial reviewer on each of the three permission-heavy ones:
  - **Name-only guests** (migration `0009_guests`):
    - review and workshop links can let people join with just a name;
    - guests get a `guest` workspace membership and only their link's board;
    - every place a name shows adds "(guest)";
    - there is a server-side refusal list, per-IP and per-link join limits, and a sweep that ends guest sessions with no board.
    
    The reviewer fixed 7 issues: parallel joins getting past the rate limit, the marker being cut off on cursors, the per-link cap bypassed through accept, IPv6 /64 keying, HEAD requests skipping the guard, guests moving boards between projects, and a cross-workspace existence leak.
  - **Private brainstorming** (`0010_private_notes`): drafts live only in Postgres and only their author can read them; events carry counts. The reveal commits once and then puts real note shapes into the board through a new sync endpoint, in one validated storage transaction, with shape ids derived from the draft ids, so the reveal is idempotent. An anonymous reveal carries no author anywhere. The reviewer fixed a cancel-during-reveal race, added a start rate limit, fixed a double submit, and handled oversized frames.
  - **Dashboard thumbnails** (`0011_board_thumbnails`): an editor's client captures a thumbnail after edits settle and on leave. The api validates it by magic bytes and dimensions, rate-limits it, and swaps it in the blob store. GET needs view permission and sends nosniff and a sandbox CSP.
  - **Export policy** (`0012_board_export_policy`), where owners pick "Viewers and commenters can export, copy and download files":
    - limited viewers get 403 `EXPORT_DISABLED` on original downloads and board export;
    - previews and media elements keep rendering;
    - the client hides the export and copy entries.
    
    The reviewer closed an import bypass and a keep-sharing duplicate bypass.
  - **Offsite backups**: each consistent board backup is also streamed to the blob store (R2 on staging) with a manifest (sha256, clock, schema, referenced asset ids). Retention is the newest 12 plus one per day for 14 days, there is `/internal/backups/status`, and there is a restore drill from the store. The runbook is in DEPLOY.md.
  - **The "Lobby tour" sample board**: six frames in presentation order, first in the gallery, with an empty-dashboard button. DEMO.md was rewritten for a 5-minute leadership demo.

- **Graceful shutdown on Render was broken, and is now fixed.** Every deploy logged `[FATAL tini] Unexpected error when forwarding signal: 'Operation not permitted'`. Render runs containers without CAP_KILL, so the root tini could not signal the app, which runs as `node`. The entrypoint now drops to `node` first and then execs tini. Reproduced and verified on the NAS with `--cap-drop KILL`: old = tini FATAL; new = the app gets SIGTERM and exits 0.
- **Wave 4b**:
  - **AI on the board** (OpenAI gpt-5.4-mini; migration `0016_ai_usage`):

    - the prompt is built from what the server reads through the sync snapshot, the output is validated with zod and mapped back only through aliases, and the result applies as one undo step;
    - hard limits, because the key has no provider cap:
      - per person: 30 requests/min and 20 calls/hour;
      - in flight: 2 per person, 3 per workspace, 4 in total;
      - daily token budgets per workspace (400k), per person (150k) and global (2M), reserved worst-case in Postgres under an advisory lock;
      - fail-closed, plus a kill switch;
    - guests are refused, and so are viewers limited by the export policy.

Step C: the dashboard, landing, History, Share dialog, invite page and sign-in read `me.access` (creators create and hand out access; outsiders see an honest "nothing shared yet" landing with their address, no create or copy controls, and a Share dialog that only lowers, removes, revokes and denies; one-time links are locked to an address, with a masked hint and a wrong-account state that never auto-accepts), plus the new e2e spec `domain-access.spec.ts` (flows 1-5). Gate on the merged `next` (E2E_PORT_BASE 6800): lint clean (1,035 files); typecheck all projects; `pnpm test` 3,297 passed / 36 skipped (293 files, 9 skipped); `domain-access.spec.ts` 5/5 three runs in a row (15/15); the whole e2e suite 331/332 in 23.8 min, the one failure being `workshop-copy` R2-09's time-of-day artefact (run at 23:29 local, the 3-hour link crossed midnight and the unchanged badge rightly showed a date), which passed 2/2 unmodified when re-run at 00:00. Details in docs/DOMAIN-ACCESS-CONTRACTS.md §18; DEPLOY.md "Domain access" already carries the §13 rollout, rollback and offboarding steps. **Not run**: the opt-in real-Postgres tests (no Postgres on this PC), the demo video and perf smoke checks. **Next**: the independent security review (§14), Clerk test mode off on the staging instance (recorded in DEPLOY.md), `DRAFTROOM_CREATOR_EMAILS` on the staging api, then the lead deploys api → sync → web.
- **Domain access, verifier findings, fixer round 1 on `next`**: an independent verification of the merged `next` found one medium and eleven low defects, all fixed (docs/DOMAIN-ACCESS-CONTRACTS.md §19, with the contract changes marked "fixer round 1"). Api: the Share dialog's reads (GET …/invitations, GET …/sharing/members) freshen the caller like /api/me, so a removed company address stops multi-use link URLs and link minting about 1 h after any of them; a Clerk exchange that finds the account banned also ends its existing sessions; a stored check time more than 60 s ahead of the clock (a deploy overlap) no longer blocks newer addresses or refreshes, and every dev login replaces the dev address; the invitation preview gains `sentByYou`. Web: an `access-outside` dashboard notice for outside accounts whose role would create (their own workspace included); the Share note says "paused" for an unconfirmed account; the outside landing no longer promises an email; an inviter opening their own locked link is told whom it is for; "pick someone from the workspace" no longer offers Owner and a row says in words why Owner is off; History and the export setting stop promising what a non-creator cannot do. Tests: api `staleness.test.ts` (10) and new cases in contracts, invite-lock and the web models; `domain-access.spec.ts` grew from 5 to 11 tests (flow 6, the creator-side canOwn UI, request access, a locked workspace invitation, failure states rendered through `page.route`); 7 of the 11 fail on the pre-fix source. Gate (E2E_PORT_BASE 6800): lint clean; typecheck all projects; `pnpm test` 3,314 passed / 36 skipped; `domain-access.spec.ts` 11/11 three runs in a row; the 13 related e2e specs 65/65. **Not run**: the whole e2e suite, the opt-in real-Postgres tests, demo and perf. `ClerkSignIn`'s error rendering stays covered by unit tests only (the e2e stack cannot load Clerk).

No api, contracts or schema change.   - An invitation to a board in the trash now previews `boardInTrash`, and the page says so. Accepting answers 409 `BOARD_IN_TRASH` with nothing redeemed and no grant, and a restore makes a still-valid link work again. Workspaces have no trash.

Present to everyone stays 3c's: it plugs in through `ported/live-slots.ts` and `presenterOf`.
  - **Export policy** (`features/sharing/ported/`) on lib/signals, the share badge; **thumbnails** (`features/thumbnails/ported/`) from the engine's export of the start view or first frame, the element schematic above 30 items, and the leave drawing from a scene snapshot.
  - **Shared modules** (both builds): `slides.ts` generic over the id; the export-policy rules moved to `sharing/export-rules.ts`; thumbnails' stand-in marks to `capture-state.ts`. A unit test fails if the ported folders' runtime imports reach tldraw.
  - **Gate** (E2E_PORT_BASE 6560/6570; other agents' runs shared the machine): lint (1,626 files); `pnpm typecheck` and the e2e tsc; `pnpm test` 4,591 passed / 36 skipped / 1 failed, the failure being `apps/worker/test/office.test.ts`'s LibreOffice timing test (not touched; 42/42 when run alone, three times); 19 new unit tests. **Ported specs** (`present-export-present`, `present-export-files`, `thumbnails-capture`, `sharing-export-control`): **21/21 three runs in a row** (1 `test.fixme`: the context-menu export waits for 3a's context-menu shell). **The default build's** `present-export`, `present-live`, `thumbnails`, `export-control`, `sharing-polish` and `dashboard-share` specs: **53/53** once. The normal build has 0 hits for the new markers (and the engine build has them). Heavy board, largest frame: SVG 378,561 bytes (one 16 KB woff2) in 40 ms (3E.md).
  - **Deviations** (15, in 3E.md), among them: the fullscreen element is the canvas region; the slide camera is the presenter's own `viewport.set` (3b's `zoomTo`/`limits` are stubs); no "Present to everyone" button until 3c plugs in; frame names drawn by the engine add height to whole-board PNGs; thumbnails' schematic draws images as tiles until 3g's `files.urlOf`.
  - **Requests, not applied:** CR 3E-2 (a member status for landed parts), CR 3E-3 (`cursorVisible` composing several providers: 3e and 3f both contribute).
- **Excalidraw migration, wave 3a (canvas tools) on `engine/w3-3a`** (from `engine/excalidraw` 5b9c87a). The ported `canvas-ux` feature (`apps/web/src/features/canvas-ux/ported/**`) puts Lobby's own chrome on the Excalidraw board page; the tldraw page and its files are unchanged.
  - **Host slots:** the toolbar (COL-07 order: Select, Hand, Note, Text, Rectangle, Pen, Connector, Frame, Media, features' main tools, Templates when navigation's panel exists; "More" with the last-used overflow tool in one extra slot), the style panel (RETRO-20/R16 visibility, COL-06 note palette, tldraw's colour, fill, dash, size, font, alignment, arrowhead and line-kind names mapped to engine fields by `style-vocabulary.ts`, the values the converter writes), the context and board menus (every feature's `contextMenu`/`mainMenu` entries in `MENU_GROUPS` order; the board menu folds `board`+`ai` into Board ▸, `clipboard`/`edit`/`arrange`/`select` into Edit ▸, `view` into View ▸, `export` into Export ▸), the keyboard shortcuts dialog (from the registries), and the selection bar (every feature's `selectionBar` items; order bands: below 100 the main row, copy link 10, lock 20, tags 30, AI 40; from 100 the arrange row, align/distribute 100, arrange as grid 200).
  - **Keys** (commands with `kbd` and deferring feature keys, pinned by `ported/keyboard-map.test.ts`): Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+L (lock, the selection kept; a frame locks what is inside it), PgUp/PgDn, Shift+PgUp/PgDn, ] [, Alt+] Alt+[, Alt+A/H/W/V/S and Alt+Shift+H/V (align, distribute), ?, Ctrl/Cmd+/ and Ctrl/Cmd+Alt+/ (shortcuts), Ctrl/Cmd+U (Media), Shift+F10 and the menu key, Alt+F10; deferring: RETRO-02 after an edit, Enter (rename a frame, A11Y-07 place a shape), Tab/Shift+Tab/Ctrl+Enter/Ctrl+Shift+Enter (the next note, RETRO-09), Delete on a selection with locked elements, Ctrl/Cmd+C/X while exporting is off.
  - **Also:** double-click on empty canvas makes a note (in the unlocked frame under it), a frame name renames on double-click or Enter, the locked hint and locked-frame badges (F8), the export gate on copy and the menus, snapping on by default (once per browser) and sans for new text, the focus guard, `registries.menusOpen`.
  - **`styles.of`/`styles.apply`** (`canvas/api/parts/styles.ts`): fields applied only where they mean something, a container's text fields on its label, one ink for a note and its label, text changes laid out again in the same transact (`redrawTextBoundingBox`, `relayoutStickyNotes`). CR-3A-1 (a "CR:" commit): the member table gains status `part` (`LANDED_PARTS`); other wave-3 owners add their paths there. CR-3A-2 (a "CR:" commit): 2C's `board-features.spec.ts` follows the toolbar slot's provider and deselects before its focus-mode click.
  - **Ported specs** (`e2e/excalidraw/canvas-ux-{toolbar,keys,menus,canvas,duplicate}.spec.ts`, 27 tests): toolbar, tools, RETRO-20, COL-06, styles, Miro keys, type-to-edit, RETRO-02, A11Y-12, RETRO-09, A11Y-07, RETRO-14, A11Y2-02, two-browser lock, context menu, FAC-07's menu half, export control's copy and menus, RETRO-21b, F8, MAK-03, A11Y2-06, MAK-02, and the onDuplicate rules (Ctrl+D, menu, Alt-drag, paste). MAK-06 is `test.fail` (CR-3A-3).
  - **Requests:** CR-3A-3 (port/2C: a transact's `index` writes never move the element in the scene array and Excalidraw renumbers every index in array order, so `tx.reorder` changes no paint order); CR-3A-4 (input layer: read-only roles cannot copy at all; let copy follow the export policy); CR-3A-5 (keymap: Alt+D, the address bar, is prevented though §2.3.3 lists it as a browser chord); CR-3A-6 (tools: `setTool('hand')` for read-only roles, and the `highlight` tool); CR-3A-7 (2C: the note and text tools ignore the next-element style, and `styles.next()` has no `strokeWidth`).
  - **Deviations** (each also in its module's header): stroke sizes are the engine's widths (1, 2, 4, 8; pen 0.5 to 4), not tldraw's; no in-place shape-type change, no elbow switch of an existing arrow, no serif; no style panel for the note tool and only a colour for the text tool (CR-3A-7); no highlighter and no Hand for read-only roles in the toolbar (CR-3A-6); Copy hidden for read-only roles (CR-3A-4); no Paste, flip, stack or stretch in the menus; align right has no key (Alt+D is the browser's); a lock of a frame also locks its contents (the engine's rule), so the hint names the frame; the export gate keeps its own policy store until 3e's ported one; F3's "dialog closed with the mouse" Space case is not handled (Lobby's buttons never take the keyboard on a click). The ported specs wait for Saved before typing into a note they just made (spike B's O2 race lost the first words once), check the toolbar order by test ids instead of tldraw's pixel snapshot, and fill rectangles they click (the engine picks a transparent shape by its outline).
  - **Gate** (E2E_PORT_BASE 6400 and 6410): lint (1,636 files); `pnpm -r typecheck` and the Node-safety tsc; `pnpm test` 4,613 passed / 36 skipped (40 new); the ported canvas-ux specs **27/27 three runs in a row** (26 passes and MAK-06's expected failure each run); the whole `e2e/excalidraw` folder once **80/80** (the 5 expected failures included); the default (tldraw) build's canvas-ux, canvas-ux.fix, canvas-ux-2, keyboard-safety, a11y-polish, a11y-2, a11y-3, export-control, canvas-2.fix and client-defaults specs once **61/61**; the normal build has none of the ported code. **Not ported here:** the zoom keys, M, Ctrl+F and every navigation feature (3b), the comment entry of the context menu (3c), exports and downloads (3e, 3g), FAC-07's label hiding (3f, 3e), phone flows (3i), and keyboard-safety's F11 undo-with-selection (2C's history).
- **Excalidraw migration, wave 3C (comments and people) on `engine/w3-3c`** (from `engine/excalidraw` 5b9c87a). The ported `comments`, `people` and `present-live` features of `CANVAS_FEATURES`, 3c's `canvas.follow` part, and their e2e specs. The default (tldraw) build keeps its own files; the only changes it sees are the two shared stores and two shared components below.
  - **Shared, engine-neutral:** the comments store and the present-live store take a `SignalsKit` (`features/comments/cells.ts`): tldraw's atoms on the tldraw page (`tldraw-cells.ts`, deleted at wave 4), lib/signals on the Excalidraw page. `Composer` and `ThreadView` read the store through its kit, so both pages use them. The store tests run every case on both kits.
  - **`canvas.follow`** (`canvas/api/parts/follow.ts`): follows a session's presence `view` (the whole view fitted and centred, re-fitted on resize); it ends on `stop()`, when that session leaves the roster, and on this person's wheel, pinch or canvas press. `followChanged` fires on each change.
  - **comments:** the comment tool (C; click to comment, drag to pan; Enter, Esc, Tab / Shift+Tab and Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+↓/↑ while it is on), pins and popovers in a scene overlay, the dock panel, the top-bar tool toggle and bell, the context-menu entry "Comment" (group `collaborate`), `?thread=` deep links, and toasts with View / Undo. Anchors keep the api's `shape:<id>` refs, so every existing thread keeps its place.
  - **people:** the top-bar item `people` (the page's own avatars step aside). It has the searchable list with each person's role and "(guest)" from the roster, Follow / Following, Bring here and Bring everyone to me through the workshops store, and a "Following Bob" label with a coloured frame and Stop following.
  - **present-live:** the live controller, the invitation, the top-bar chip, and `LiveTop` / `LiveBarControls` for 3e's presentation chrome. It runs on present mode's presenter, which 3e provides with `provideLivePresenter(ctx.registries, presenter)` (`features/present-export/live/ported/presenter.ts`, the seam). Until 3e does, present-live shows nothing.
  - **Change requests (two "CR:" commits):** the member table gets status `part` for a landed wave-3 part (`LANDED_PARTS` in `canvas/api/impl.ts`; the contracts test's `followChanged` stub check, and the table generator). The board session (`canvas/board/session.ts`, 2C's) re-sends presence on a pan or zoom, so followers see a view that is current without a pointer move.
  - **Requested, not applied:** CR-3C-1: add a follow target to presence (`PresenceIn`/`PeerPresence`, relayed by the room). Without it nobody can see who follows them by camera, and the server cannot keep the followed peer inside the 40-cursor cap. CR-3C-2: 3e provides its presenter and renders `LiveTop` / `LiveBarControls`. For 3a: `keyConflicts` needs a tool qualifier, because comments claims Enter, Esc, Tab, Shift+Tab and Mod+Shift+↓/↑ only while the comment tool is on. For 3f: port the summon handler ("Bring here", "Gather everyone") onto `canvas.follow.start`, with R2-04's readable follow.
  - **Deviations (by design):**
    - A new point anchor carries no page id (one page). A legacy one is shifted by `lb_doc.import.legacyPages`, and a thread never says "(another page)".
    - The comment toasts are the board's notices, with the same words and actions.
    - The people item and the live chip sit among the feature controls, after the Saved and connection pills. On phones the people button stays in the bar, without avatars.
    - "N following you", "Following you" and "Following Ann" come only from a live presentation (CR-3C-1).
    - Follow ends when the followed session leaves; tldraw kept following the same user across their reload. The camera moves at once, with no easing.
    - Roles come from the roster, not from GET members.
    - The comment tool's cursor is a crosshair.
    - Tab steps in plain reading order (top to bottom, then left to right) among the selection's siblings.
    - A wheel over a pin does not pan the canvas.
    - The dock tab's hint reads "C".
  - **Gate** (`E2E_PORT_BASE` 6480 for the Excalidraw specs, 6490 for the default build; the machine was shared with other agents' runs):
    - lint (1,625 files); `pnpm typecheck` (every project), e2e tsc and Node-safety tsc.
    - `pnpm test` 4,635 passed / 36 skipped. Each full run had one to two timing failures in apps/worker (`remote.test.ts` lease heartbeats, `office.test.ts` size cap) or apps/sync (`protocol/presence.test.ts`). All three files pass alone (83/83), and this branch changes nothing in apps/worker, apps/sync, apps/api or packages. The 3c unit tests: follow part 6, ported live controller 10, comments geometry and keys 11, people presence 3, and the two stores on both kits.
    - **The ported specs 39/39 three runs in a row:** comments-core 17, comments-review 9, comments-privacy 1, comments-notify 2, people-list 4, present-live 6. Ten of them are expected failures (`test.fail`): CR 2F-2's restore, CR-3C-1, 3f's summon, 3a's context menu, and 3e's presenter for the six present-live tests.
    - The whole `e2e/excalidraw` folder once: 92/92, with 2C's `board-presence` using the new people item and the four known wave-2 expected failures.
    - The default build's specs of these features once, 71/71: comments, comment-privacy, notify-comments, present-live, present-export, facilitation-review.fix, keyboard-safety, phone-overlays, shell-sharing-platform.fix.
    - A normal build carries none of the ported strings.
  - **Not done here:** the context-menu, toolbar and shortcuts shells that render comments' entries (3a); the presenter seam's other side (3e); the summon handler and the phone readable follow (3f); a legacy-page e2e on a converted board (unit-tested only).
- **Excalidraw migration, wave 3G (media) on `engine/w3-3g`** (from `engine/excalidraw` 5b9c87a; `docs/research/excalidraw/wave3/3G.md`). The ported `media` feature (`file-card/ported/**`) on the canvas API, beside the unchanged tldraw code.
  - **Files are `image` elements** holding only the version id (`fileId`) and the stable facts (`customData.lobby.file`); live facts come from the api's meta. A card's face is today's card, drawn by a scene overlay (same copy and test ids); opaque elements above a card cut holes in it. Images and placed pages show Lobby's "Processing…" tile until the loader hands them over, "Couldn't process this image" when the worker refused them.
  - **Paste, drop and Ctrl+U** go through the `media.files` content handler: limits, tracked uploads (Saved waits), today's drop layout (grids, frames, template slots with a centre crop, the rest under the frame, one scale for images and cards), one undo step for a drop's images. The PDF viewer and Place on board, double-click to open a card's original, Download original.
  - **Canvas side** (`canvas/api/parts/files.ts`): `files.urlOf`, `files.insert`, the upload tracker handed to the media feature (`assets/board-media.ts`), and the derivative swap (`canvas/files/swap.ts`: `thumb` → `display`, local only). "CR:" 3G-1 adds a `part` status to the member table for landed wave-3 parts.
  - **Specs:** 22 ported tests in `e2e/excalidraw/media-*.spec.ts` (files, video, viewer, office, drops): **22/22 three runs in a row** (the first as part of the whole `e2e/excalidraw` folder, 75 passed). The tldraw specs are unchanged; the default build's media spec files passed once, **43/43**. Lint, typecheck and `pnpm test` (4,595 passed, 22 new) pass.
  - **Deviations** (3G.md §2): no hint props ("Loading" until the meta answers), no local preview while an image uploads, a drop's images land together, new elements go on top of a slot, GIFs show their first frame (the plan defers playback), the export policy reader is the media feature's own until 3e lands.
  - **Requests:** CR 3G-2 (`LobbyFiles.track`), O2 (keep the local `@display` suffix on a server rebuild), 2C (the engine's files map can keep a revoked `blob:` URL), 3a (the context-menu and selection-bar shells show Download original), 3e (the export policy reader).
- **Excalidraw migration, wave 3b (navigation) on `engine/w3-3b`** (from `engine/excalidraw` 5b9c87a; `docs/research/excalidraw/wave3/3B.md`). The ported `navigation` feature (`features/canvas-ux/ported/navigation/**`) is on the Excalidraw board page, on `canvas/api` only. The tldraw build is unchanged except the dashboard's content-search links, which keep `?d=` in the default build.
  - **Built:**
    - Lobby's camera: `viewport.zoomTo`, `centerOn`, `ensureVisible`, `zoomStep` and `limits`, going below Excalidraw's 0.1 down to the whole-board fit. Change request CR 3b-1 (a `CR:` commit) makes them `composed` in the member table.
    - The zoom keys and zoom menu, the Lobby-drawn minimap (closed by default), and the navigation host slot with phone-width Zoom to fit.
    - Board search, the Frames panel (`customData.lobby.present`), and the Templates picker.
    - Copy link with `?el=` and `?view=` links and the legacy `?d=` reader (`lib/deep-link.ts`), and `?el=` dashboard content-search links on the new board page.
    - The first view (template, links, the last camera with v1 cameras read through the frozen page offsets, `lb_doc.startView`, fit or first frame) and PRE-08.
    - Arrange as grid, select by type, and paste as sticky notes.
  - **Blocked, built and marked:** the template insert (CR-2D-2 `LobbyTx.insert`; its 2 specs are `test.fail`).
  - **Deviations** (3B.md §3):
    - only owners can set the start view (canvas-schema `startView` is an owner key);
    - a locked frame keeps its presentation place (the room refuses `customData` on locked elements);
    - pasted notes use the new-note face;
    - the dashboard template insert is an undo step;
    - the first view waits about 150 ms for the board to settle.
  - **Found** (3B.md §4):
    - `port-impl.ts` drops an update to an element created in the same `transact` (so does 2C's `tx.duplicate` structure write);
    - a first visit within 5 s of an `lb_doc` write gets the old doc (the room's cached reset snapshot; pinned by a `test.fail` spec);
    - features mount before the board's content is in;
    - `viewport.set` is not read back at once.
  - **Requested:** CR 3b-2 (wheel and pinch deltas on the input stream, for the wheel below 0.1), CR 3b-3 (`startView` editor-writable), CR 3b-4 (`present` writable on locked frames).
  - **Not ported:** the wheel below 0.1, the level-of-detail pass, the UI clicks on 3a's shells (the specs run the same entries through the page's dev-only registry hook), search by tag (3d), and F15's upload e2e (3g).
  - **Gate** (`E2E_PORT_BASE` 6440 and 6450; the machine was shared with other agents' runs):
    - lint, typecheck (15 projects), engine-probe, e2e and Node-safety tsc;
    - `pnpm test` 4,609 passed / 36 skipped (36 new);
    - the ported specs `e2e/excalidraw/navigation-*.spec.ts` **22/22 three runs in a row** (3 of them `test.fail`: the 2 inserts blocked by CR-2D-2, and the stale-doc defect);
    - the default-build specs of these features (`canvas-ux`, `canvas-ux-2`, `canvas-ux.fix`, `search`) **36/36 once**;
    - the normal build and the engine-flag build without the guard carry no ported navigation code.
- **Excalidraw migration, wave 3d (mind maps + tags) on `engine/w3-3d`** (from `engine/excalidraw` 5b9c87a; `docs/research/excalidraw/wave3/3D.md`). On the Excalidraw board page (the engine build), mind maps and tags now work; the tldraw page is unchanged in behaviour.
  - **Mind maps** (`features/mindmap/ported/`): the tree in `customData.lobby.mindmap` / `mindmapEdge` (element ids, validated on read), rectangle topics with labels and bound elbow arrows; every operation (new map, start from a shape, child, sibling, up a level, collapse, Tidy up, delete a branch) is one `transact`, the keeper re-lays out after resizes, text changes, a dragged centre and drops, joining the change's undo step. **Collapse as decided in spike B (B12):** only `collapsed` is stored; every client hides the branch through `canvas.hide` (render override, hit guard, Ctrl+A, box/lasso post-filter, exports), and the layout keeps the collapsed branch's area free. Keys (Tab/Enter/Shift+Tab, arrows, F2, Delete, Alt+Shift+M) as feature keys and commands; keys typed while a new topic's lease is pending are held and replayed in order. The topic bar, badges, delete confirmation, context-menu entries, the `mindmap` tool and the outline (Markdown/CSV, under the export policy).
  - **Tags** (`features/tags/ported/`): `tag:<key>` on elements and lb_doc `tagDef:<key>` (definition first, elements in one `transact`); chips in a scene overlay layer (one camera transform, LOD by zoom); the picker (3a's selection-bar item and context-menu entry, or the `tags.picker` command), the Tags dock panel, "Show only" as a veil over the canvas, and search/CSV helpers for 3b and 3e. After an undo the lb_doc list follows the elements' copies.
  - **Shared modules** (both builds): `tags/tag-core.ts` and `mindmap/outline-core.ts` hold the engine-neutral rules (the tldraw modules re-export them). A unit test fails if either ported folder's runtime imports reach tldraw.
  - **Gate** (E2E_PORT_BASE 6520; other agents' runs shared the machine): lint (1,638 files); `pnpm -r typecheck` (15 projects, the e2e tsc included); `pnpm test` 4,602 passed / 36 skipped (402 files; 29 new unit tests, one of them a walk that fails if the ported folders' runtime imports reach tldraw). **Ported specs** (`e2e/excalidraw/mindmap-keyboard`, `mindmap-structure`, `tags-picker`, `tags-panel`): **15/15 three runs in a row** (each run also 1 skipped: search by tag and the CSV column wait for 3b and 3e to use the helpers; the guest tag creation is a `test.fail` for CR-3D-5 and failed as expected; no gesture needed its retry). **The default build's** `tests/mindmap.spec.ts` and `tests/tags.spec.ts`: **15/15** once. Earlier gate runs failed on keyboard races under load; the causes found with in-page logs and fixed are listed in 3D.md (Flakes).
  - **Deviations** (14, in 3D.md), among them: a new topic's words are their own undo step (W1-9 `mergeTop`); F2, not type-to-edit, on a selected topic (letters are tool keys); the map follows a dragged centre on the drop; chips are above every element; "Show only" is a veil; undoing a tag's creation leaves it listed (lb_doc is not undone); a guest cannot create a tag (CR-3D-5).
  - **Requests, not applied** (no `CR:` commits): CR-3D-1 (port-impl drops updates of elements created in the same transact), CR-3D-2 (synthetic key-downs / a pending key scope / the new-element lease path for feature-created elements), CR-3D-3 (type-to-edit on a topic), CR-3D-4 (derived arrow changes recorded as undo steps), CR-3D-5 (editor guests writing `tagDef:`); for 3a (render the tags selection-bar item and the menu entries, a toolbar action hook, Delete via `mindmap.delete`), 3b (search by tag through `SearchTagFilter`), 3e (`tagsCsvCells`, the policy store for the outline).
- **Excalidraw migration, wave 3H (history and AI) on `engine/w3-3h`** (from `engine/excalidraw` 5b9c87a; docs/research/excalidraw/wave3/3H.md). Both features now run on the Excalidraw board page beside their tldraw versions, which the default build still ships unchanged.
  - **History** (`features/history/ported/`): the dock panel with the same copy, rules and test ids (checkpoints, owners delete, restore as a new board, duplicate, `.json` export, "Recently deleted" for editors). The preview is a bitmap drawn with the engine's exporter and Lobby's own pan and zoom (`canvas/preview`), never a second editor, with the checkpoint's words in a hidden text layer. It opens on the first frame when the whole board would be unreadable.
  - **AI** (`features/ai/ported/`): themes, summaries, action items and ideas, each applied as one `transact`, with `customData.lobby.ai` and an `ai-*` kind on everything AI makes. The popover, notice, busy pill, summary panel and the Undo toast that only undoes the result work as before. The entry points are registry entries for 3a's selection bar and menus. Until 3a's selection bar lands, a stand-in bar over the selection carries the AI button.
  - **Change requests:** CR-3H-1 is a "CR:" commit: `canvas/api/index.ts` gains `loadCheckpointPreview`. CR-3H-2 is requested: a label's `lastEditedBy` is stamped when it is created, so a template's prompt notes count as typed (R9). CR-3H-3 is requested: the port's `transact` drops an update to an element created in the same step. Restores and the reveal checkpoint wait for CR 2F-2; their specs are `test.fail`.
  - **Tests:** 14 new unit tests. 26 ported specs in `e2e/excalidraw` (`history-checkpoints`, `history-deleted`, `history-copies`, `ai-actions`); 5 of them are expected failures that name their CR.
  - **Not ported:** the backup restore drill (backups are off on this engine until 2B), and the image and PDF-card parts of the history specs (3g).
- **Excalidraw migration, wave 3i (a11y + mobile) on `engine/w3-3i`** (from `engine/excalidraw` 5b9c87a; plan §4.8). The ported `a11y` feature (`features/a11y/ported/**`) on the Excalidraw board page:
  - **Live region** (`liveRegion` slot): says what is selected at rest ("Sticky note: Faster builds. 2 of 5", "… 1 of 2 in Retro", "Group of 3 shapes", "3 shapes selected"), for clicks, keys and api selections; Alt+R repeats; quiet while a note is typed in; stays in focus mode.
  - **Keyboard traversal:** F6 from anywhere gives the board the keyboard (selecting the first item in reading order when nothing is selected, as tldraw's "Move focus to canvas" did); Alt+→/↓ and Alt+←/↑ walk the reading order (tldraw 5.4.2's algorithm; one level per frame; a group is one stop; collapsed items skipped); Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+↓/↑ go into a frame and back out; Enter edits through the lease. "Skip to canvas" enters the same way (`ui/SkipLink.tsx` dispatches `draftroom:skip-to-canvas`). The canvas that takes the keyboard is named "<title> board" with the application role and a key description, and follows a rename (A11Y2-09). `traversal.ts` is the replacement for `selectAdjacentShape`/`selectFirstChildShape`/`selectParentShape` other owners (3c's comment tool) import.
  - **Content list:** a "Contents" dock panel: notes, frames (with what is inside them) and texts in reading order, one Tab stop with ↑/↓/Home/End, Enter selects on the board and keeps the keyboard in the list.
  - **Phone sheet** (`phoneSheet` slot): at 600 px and less, "Board actions" opens a bottom sheet with the role's tools (Lobby's built-ins and every feature tool with a toolbar entry), Undo/Redo and Zoom to fit; 44 px targets; Escape, Close or a press outside shut it and give the keyboard back.
  - **Palette contrast** (`canvas/a11y/palette-contrast.test.ts`, under `canvas/` because only `canvas/**` may import `@excalidraw/*`): note ink meets 4.5:1 on all 13 fills in light mode; through Excalidraw's dark filter every note but **red (3.43:1)** does. The palette's owners decide the fix.

No frozen canvas-API file, no lockfile, workspace or catalog change.
  - **V1's re-check of beff59d (engine core):** a push in flight when the page reloads or a tab closes is now kept in the offline queue as a lost push (`OfflineEntry.lost`, written as soon as a flush sends it and on pagehide/hidden/unmount, which the board page now does) and the adopting page decides it as a reconnect does: no replay over another user's later write, no revived delete (V1 R4/R5, P12/P13). The lost-push rule counts writes as well as values (a socket delivers a prefix of its pushes; the room bumps a version once per changing push): a push that never arrived and one foreign write → replayed as an offline change (R8, no silent drop); a landed push set back by someone else → the later write stays (ABA, R1/R2). Lost doc writes go again only if the server holds what they replaced (R3/R7); unsent and lost doc writes are kept apart in the queue (`lostDoc`), and two tabs' queues merge their `#doc` keys (`offline-merge.ts`, P16). The board page holds Saved while keys wait in a pending editor (P1, `holdSaved`). The socket pings after 15 s of silence and drops a socket that stays silent (half-open window). Still undecidable without the room's help: a push that never arrived followed by two or more foreign writes keeps the server copy (silently); doc keys have no version (ABA possible). Request below.
  - **Also fixed:** DEF-3F-1 (a reset takes the welcome's board document, not the 5 s cached snapshot's); W5-02's consumer side (a redacted tombstone keeps the content this tab held, so an undo of one's own delete revives in place).

- **Excalidraw migration, wave 3b owner 3CF (people + workshops) on `engine/w3b-3cf`**: R2-04 and CR-3F-3, CR-3F-4. Three expected-failure marks come off.
  - **CR-3F-3, the follow camera hook** (commit `CR:`; `docs/EXCALIDRAW-CONTRACTS.md` §12 W3-10, for the contracts owner's review): `LobbyFollow.start(sid, {camera})`. The rule gets the followed view, this canvas's size and the default fit, and returns a camera or null. 3c's `parts/follow.ts` applies it on every view and resize, falls back to the fit on null, a non-finite camera or a throw, and replaces the options when the session already followed is started again. The change is additive: the member table is unchanged.
  - **R2-04, the readable follow for a gathered phone:** "Gather everyone" and the people list's "Bring here" and "Bring everyone to me" use `readableFollowCamera` (`workshops/ported/camera.ts`). It applies the tldraw board's `readableFollowViewport` rule: a much narrower screen sees the leader's view centre at the leader's zoom, at most 0.5. Presence carries only the leader's scene rectangle, so the summon now carries the summoner's canvas size (`@draftroom/contracts` `SummonScreen`, optional in `SummonInput` and `WorkshopSummonEvent`; the api validates and relays it; the tldraw board never sends it). The follower takes the leader's zoom as that canvas height over the view's height, so a dock panel the leader opens later (which changes only the width) does not change it. A window resize by the leader after the summon does; a zoom in presence would remove this (requested).
  - **CR-3F-4, note authors:** decided that the feature drawing the overlays on notes owns the label, and implemented as the workshops scene layer `workshops-authors` (`ported/NoteAuthors.tsx`, `note-authors.ts`). It draws the first name from the note label's server-stamped `lobbyServer.lastEditedBy` in the note's bottom right corner. The layer uses scene units under one camera transform, rotates with the note and goes through Excalidraw's dark filter. It is left out under an opaque element painted above that corner (a grid lookup), outside the note's frame, on a collapsed branch, on the note being typed in, below zoom 0.28, and while "Hide note authors" is on. It is shown while presenting. Deviations: no hover tooltip (the layer takes no pointer events), and exports never draw it.
  - **Specs:** the marks are off in `workshops-follow` and `a11y-phone-overlays` (R2-04) and in `workshops-facilitation` (CR-3F-4, which now also checks the corner, that the label follows the note, and that it hides and comes back). The private-notes reveals check the labels: the named reveal shows two labels with Alice's name, both hidden by the setting; the anonymous reveal shows none. Spec fix: the "Private ideas" frame test starts its two brainstorms as a host of its own (`dana`). Since the reveals pass, alice's earlier starts fill the api's 10-starts-a-minute limit (2 of 2 runs failed before the fix).

- **R0 fixer round 1 (verifier findings, data and regression lenses) on `r0/bridge`; not deployed.**
  - **Fixed (sync)**: run-all's backups are held 90 days outside local and offsite retention (`holdUntil`, signed; the final pre-cut-over set no longer ages out after 12 newer backups); every run-all answer comes from a run started after the call, and the report says `inMaintenance`; seed, deleted/restore and place-notes re-check maintenance once the body is read; a staged restore over a converted board carries its lb_* tables, indexes and counters into the restored file unchanged (digest-checked, else not applied); offsite manifests keep entries in a format R0 does not know (the drill and restore refuse them by name); asset-refs of a converted board says `newCanvasTables: true`.
  - **Fixed (api/web)**: the asset collector counts a v2 checkpoint (row or body) as unread and never records it as scanned, and a converted board as not fully read (passes incomplete, nothing new marked or deleted); a sync snapshot without `documents` is refused as SYNC_UNAVAILABLE (no empty checkpoint, duplicate or export while a rollback's deploys overlap); the unchanged check compares with the newest v1 checkpoint only; a `new_canvas` board gets no ticket (409 NEW_CANVAS) and the web shows the message without a socket; the refusal screen offers "Go to your boards" instead of Retry and says what the switch is.

- **R0 fixer round 2 (the regression verifier's low items 1-3) on `r0/bridge`; not deployed.**
  - **run-all answers at once** (sync): `POST /internal/backups/run-all` answers 202 with a run id, the run goes on in the server, and `GET /internal/backups/run-all/<runId>` has its progress and, once done, the report (`GET /internal/backups/run-all` lists the runs; the newest 20 are kept until sync restarts). Before, the report came only at the end of the one POST, and Node's `fetch` gives up after 300 s without response headers, so a long run lost its answer and no retry could get it back. The fresh-run coalescing is unchanged (a call during a run gets the next run), and a shutdown stops a run after its current board. The runbook command `node apps/sync/src/backup/run-all-cli.ts [--expect-maintenance] [--run <runId>]` polls with short requests and prints the report on stdout.
  - **A manual checkpoint of every board** (api, §7.5 step 4): `POST /internal/checkpoints/run-all` (internal secret; exempt from the Origin check by its route pattern only) saves an authorless manual checkpoint "Before the move to the new canvas" of every board, any workspace and status, read from sync's snapshot route. It is `unchanged` on a repeat (same name, content and clock), `skipped` for boards made on the new canvas, and `failed` per board without stopping the others. It answers and polls like run-all; the command is `node apps/api/src/features/history/checkpoint-all-cli.ts`. Both commands end without `process.exit()`: on Windows an exit while fetch sockets closed aborted the process in libuv after the report (found in the first real-Postgres run, a683157).
  - **DEPLOY.md** "R0 at the cut-over and in a rollback" now has every step §7.5 and §5.9 put there. Cut-over: announce the window and close private brainstorms; maintenance on (runtime only: a sync restart turns it off); the final run-all with `--expect-maintenance`, its report kept off the service; then the checkpoint of every board, whose `documentClock`s must equal run-all's; then the new build. Rollback: maintenance on and the lb_* exports to `rollback-exports/` on the Excalidraw build first, then R0's api, sync and web.
  - **Copy**: the History line reads "Made on the new canvas. Preview and restore it when Lobby is back on the new canvas." The dashboard card shows "Opens again when Lobby is back on the new canvas." under the "New canvas" badge, on screen (the tooltip is gone). The refusal messages and the refusal screen say the same, and nothing on screen says "after the switch" any more.
  - **For the Excalidraw branch** (not changed here): the plan's "Contracts R0 fixed" item 2 says `boards.new_canvas` is true for boards "created or converted" on the new canvas. R0 refuses a `new_canvas` board's ticket, so a converted board marked that way would not open during a rollback. Converted boards must keep `false`, as DEPLOY.md says.
  - **Tests added**: sync `run-all-async.test.ts` (8: views, queueing, failure, shutdown, retention of runs, a POST answered before a run longer than the client waits, the CLI's exits) and a real-process CLI test in `run-all.test.ts`; the run-all callers in `run-all`, `final-backup-hold`, `maintenance-body`, `lb-identity` and `maintenance` tests read the report by run id; api `features/history/checkpoint-all.test.ts` (6); web `dashboard/new-canvas-card.test.ts` (2); e2e `bridge-r0.spec.ts` checks the History line and the dashboard card (the api answers extended through `page.route`, since this stack cannot make a v2 checkpoint or a new-canvas board), and the refusal screen's new words.
  - **Real Postgres 18** (throwaway NAS container `draftroom-pgtest`, postgres:18 = 18.6 pulled for the run, `--memory=1g`, `192.168.10.1:55432` only, `max_connections=300`, data on the writable layer with a 16 MB tmpfs over `/var/lib/postgresql`, fsync/synchronous_commit/full_page_writes off, a random password in a 0600 file on the NAS). Two rounds on a683157, from the repo root, `vitest run --project api --maxWorkers=8` (the domain-access method): **1065 passed / 1 skipped (live R2), 106 files passed / 1 skipped, both rounds**; `vitest run --project packages packages/db`: **43/43 (3 files), both rounds**. Three runs without `--maxWorkers` (63 workers on this 64-core PC) failed 29, 69 and (stopped) tests with `out of shared memory` in the per-test migrations: the lock table (64 × 300 slots) cannot hold that many parallel migrations, so `--maxWorkers=8` is part of the method. The first of those runs also found the CLI exit crash above. Afterwards the container, the image and the password file were removed; the NAS lists no `draftroom-pgtest` container, no `postgres:18` image and no password file, and no other container, image, network or volume was touched.

The failure is `apps/worker/test/office.test.ts` "an output that grows past the size cap is stopped while it is being written" (35.6 MB against the 32 MB cap under this PC's load; no worker file changed); alone it failed once and passed once. e2e once: `bridge-r0` 3/3, `backups-2` 2/2, `offsite-backups` 2/2, `history` 13/13 (20/20, 2.6 min).

- **Spike B, the verifiers' fix round on `spike/excalidraw`** (fixer acting for O1, O2, O4 and the lead; `docs/research/excalidraw/spike-b/M3-fixes.md`). Every high and medium defect and every WRONG verdict of V1, V2 and V3 on `37bdfa9` is fixed, each with a regression test that failed there and passes now; most lows too. Data loss: a push whose ack was lost no longer overwrites a later write or revives a deleted element; old offline replays get STALE; board-document writes survive a reload; undo survives a retried push. Security: the text lease also holds against the bind group; a container keeps its one label; `hello` floods are cut off. Conversion: O4's roll-forward key fix is merged, the drill and staging drill run, and the leak check finds escaped, wrapped and partial copies. One change request (`CONTRACTS_REV` 6: percent-encoded signed-URL names).

  - The verifiers' probes, 3 runs of 20 repetitions each on the merged tree: all pass. B8's rows, staging included, 3 runs on the merged tree (as O4): all pass, leak check 0. Every other spike-b spec once: all pass (34 invocations; the whole L6/L10 spec 580/580 rows; B8's own port guard sent b8-restore and b8-screens to O4's ports, where they pass). Not a quiet window; the verifiers' own re-checks and V3's confirmation of the staging rows are still to come.

- **Excalidraw wave 5, owner 2A (sync room; contracts owner for the frozen canvas-schema files), branch `engine/w5-2a` from `engine/excalidraw` f336ae4.** Contracts: `CONTRACTS_REV` 7 (`b3ce1a7`) and 8 (`cb030b2`), change log W5-1 and W5-2 in `docs/EXCALIDRAW-CONTRACTS.md` §12.
  - **W5-02 (security blocker), tombstones carry no content to any role:** `hash.ts` hashes a deleted element by `TOMBSTONE_KEYS` (id, type, isDeleted, version, versionNonce, index, updated); `elements.ts` `redactTombstone()` is the wire form (a valid `WireElement` with neutral values: no text, author, customData, link, file id, geometry or membership). The room sends it in the reset snapshot (one for every role), catch-ups, every `c` op of a deleted element and a refusal's `el`. A delete reaches the sessions that held the element live as its exact patch (rev 8: rev 7's `life`-only patch let a mind map's lines come back unbound after an undo); an undelete is the full `c` op. The server keeps full rows, so "Recently deleted" and its restore, and an undo of one's own delete, work as before. Plan §2.4.2 revised.
  - **W5-05:** a locked element moved by exactly its frame's move in the same push moves with the frame (`pos`, a label's `layout`, a linear element's `path`, within 0.5 px); a frame op that does not land takes its members' moves back. Every frame of all seven templates drags whole and Saved (`e2e/excalidraw/sync-room-frames.spec.ts`).
  - **V2's re-check:** element sockets per user capped (12 per board, 128 per process); welcomes fair-queued per user and charged to a per-user row budget (200,000 rows, 20,000/s), so a viewer's catch-up flood no longer delays other joins (115 ms against 1.6-7 s on f336ae4); leases capped per user (8) as well as per tab; the guest cap counts a guest's tombstones and survives a room reopen (D5: per uid, live and deleted); a server write may leave no new invariant violation on a neighbour or a group; a relayed drag ends 60 s after its start; the signed-URL check fails closed on malformed escapes and knows `X-Goog-*` (CR).
  - **V3's low:** the index cache holds the tombstones' keys: a push never stores a live element at a tombstone's key, and a server write at one moves the tombstone.
  - **D3:** `fontFamily` 7 (Lilita One) allowed; its metrics measured in Chromium and merged into the server text-metrics table (`CALIBRATE_FAMILIES=7`; 5, 6, 8 unchanged).
  - **R0's contracts on the new canvas (api):** `canvas-engine.ts` asks the sync which engine it runs (new `GET /internal/canvas`, both engines; R0's sync answers 404 = tldraw). On the new canvas every board the api creates (new, template, duplicate, restore as new, import) is `new_canvas = true` (converted boards keep false), every checkpoint stores `body_version` from its body (2 for v2), "unchanged" compares within one body version, R0's refusals of new-canvas boards and v2 checkpoints do not apply and no answer carries R0's `newCanvas` marks. With a tldraw sync nothing changes (R0's tests unchanged).
  - **Open item 11:** `DRAFTROOM_BOOT_MAINTENANCE=1` starts sync (either engine) in maintenance; `POST /internal/maintenance {on}` switches it. Plan §7.5 step 6 updated.
  - **For other owners:** 2C: a client that holds a deleted element's content may keep it when a redacted `c` op arrives (only `TOMBSTONE_KEYS` are authoritative), which avoids a neutral flash when an undo of a delete follows a reconnect; `canvas/export/fonts.ts` still lists [5, 6, 8]; the paste mapping of other font families. 2B: `tldraw-import` `lineHeightOf` says 1.25 for every family (Lilita One's is 1.15), and V3's medium (the roll-forward's INDEX_DUPLICATE on a key shared with a tombstone; the room no longer creates the share). 2E: the spike-b stack specs need ports 7850-7999 (`e2e/spike-b/support/ports.ts`), outside this owner's 6400-6439, so only the port-less contracts check ran. - **Excalidraw wave 5, owner 3b's fixes of the early review (`docs/research/excalidraw/REVIEW-W5-EARLY.md`) on `engine/w5f-3b`** (from `engine/excalidraw` f336ae4). The review ran on 1809cf3, before wave 3b, so each finding was first re-measured on f336ae4.
  - **Fixed:**
    - W5-09: the dashboard sidebar no longer reads Trash, Archived, All boards, Recent after a board visit. The ported navigation's `.dr-nav*` rules (flex-direction: column-reverse) stayed loaded after the in-app navigation and overrode the dashboard's. The classes are now `dr-cnav*`. `navigation/css-scope.test.ts` fails any rule of a `**/ported/**` stylesheet that selects only by class names a shared stylesheet uses, unless a `[data-lobby-…]` attribute or a ported-only class scopes it. All the other ported stylesheets pass it.
    - W5-19: the jump the review measured (6.9% → 10% on a wheel out, → 16.9% on a wheel in) was the engine's wheel, which wave 3b replaced below 0.1 (W3-11). What was left was a camera below the floor, for example a board that shrank after a fit. Excalidraw's wheel snapped it in to 0.1, Ctrl+- stepped it up to the floor, and the engine's +0.1 step ran in. Now no zoom-out gesture zooms in, a step in from below goes to the floor, and the wheel below 0.1 steps by 10% of the zoom.
    - W5-21 (3b's part): under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` every move of 3b's camera members (`zoomTo`, `zoomStep`, `centerOn`, `ensureVisible`, `fit`) and the navigation feature's own `viewport.set` calls (go to frame, a phone frame) is immediate. That covers Ctrl+=/-, the zoom menu, Alt+1/2, fit, go to frame, search results, the minimap, the phone sheet's Zoom to fit and 3i's Alt+Arrow. `ensureVisible` now keeps the zoom for an item that fits the view (tldraw's `zoomToSelectionIfOffscreen` keeps it too) and zooms out only for one larger than the view.
    - W5-33 (3b's part): the board menu's View submenu (zoom in, zoom out, 100%, fit, selection, the minimap). §4.1's "Board ▸ Edit ▸ View" is now true.
    - W5-39: a connector or line selected by a `?el=` link or a search result gets Lobby's outline, which lasts while that selection stands. The engine draws neither a box nor handles for a lone two-point linear element it did not select itself.
    - W5-54: "Back to content" (tldraw's helper button). It appears a second after the view holds none of the board's content and brings the selection, or else all content, back at no more than the current zoom.
    - W5-58: `?template=` inserts without selecting, so the live region no longer says "6 shapes selected" on a board where nothing is selected.
  - **Already fixed since wave 3b, now with checks:** W5-06's zoom-to-fit part (Zoom to fit over Select at 390-412 px, the board menu under the panels button) and W5-46's (the zoom control over Select at 720 × 450 with a dock panel open). `navigation-keys` PRI-13 checks with `elementFromPoint` that each control's centre is that control.

The theme boot is `public/theme-boot.js` (a blocking classic head script, as plan §2.12 decided; `theme.test.ts` runs the file against the controller and asserts no inline script). zod is jitless through `public/zod-jitless.js`, a classic head script too: `z.config({ jitless: true })` as `main.tsx`'s first import still logged one `script-src eval` violation per page (the first harness run), and so did a module-script entry of its own (the second), because Vite merges a page's module scripts into one entry whose shared chunk (zod together with the api's schemas) is evaluated, probe included, before the entry's own statements; zod keeps its config at `globalThis.__zod_globalConfig` (`??=`), so a classic script sets it before any bundle runs (`lib/zod-jitless.test.ts` runs the file against zod in both orders).

Sign-in (dev provider), the dashboard and a board driven by hand (a note with N and a click, typing, Escape, Saved; a free text by double-click; a wheel zoom and a pan; a reload): **0 violations, 0 CSP console messages, 0 foreign requests, 0 report-uri bodies, three runs in a row** (runs 3, 4 and 5 on 6430-6432; every engine font request answered 200 from `/excalidraw-assets/`). Not covered here: Clerk's real origins (P5: a real sign-in at the rehearsal) and the static site's own header (C13, set by hand).
  - **LEAD-1 (`vite dev`, React StrictMode: the board never connected):** two causes. The board session was made in render and disposed for good by StrictMode's effect cleanup (its transport closed, 0 attempts); `LobbyBoardPage.tsx` now makes it in the effect that disposes it (`LobbyBoardView` owns the session, `LobbyBoardScreen` renders it), so the rerun gets a fresh session. Then the page connected but never Saved: the offline queue's Web Lock (`sync/adapter/offline-idb.ts`, 2C's) was requested by the first session and released before the grant, when `releaseLock` was still a no-op, so the disposed session kept the lock and the live session's `load()` never resolved; the grant now gives the lock up at once when `release()` came first (`offline-idb.test.ts`, with a fake Web Locks API that grants asynchronously like the browser; fails before). Check: `e2e/excalidraw-builds/dev-strictmode.spec.ts` under the Vite dev server (`pnpm --filter @draftroom/e2e excalidraw:dev`): the board reaches `ready`, the live session's transport has 1 attempt and is open, a note is Saved and is there after a reload; on 5caee00 it times out at the connect.
  - **LEAD-2 (`?template=` consumed by a failed insert):** the address bar's param and the tab's "inserted" marker are consumed only once the insert went in (`ported/navigation/start-view.ts`); a failed insert keeps both, the notice offers **Try again** (`template-insert-retry`), and a reload retries. A viewer, a board with content, an unknown template or a tab that inserted already still drop the param at once. `start-view.test.ts` (fails before: the param was gone after the failure).

The core keeps the server versions each ack reports and decides a lost push from the version its last acked predecessor left (`Sent.ahead`, `effectivePv`, `lost-pushes.test.ts`); the offline queue stores the corrected `pv` for a reload. A superseded group is reported (`superseded` event, the board page's notice "Someone else changed an object you edited while you were reconnecting"). The heartbeat pings on a fixed cadence and drops the socket when a ping stays unanswered, so a dead upstream behind other users' arriving frames is found in 30 s, not never (V1 medium H2; `socket.test.ts`).

- **Excalidraw wave 4b, owner 2C (web engine), branch `engine/w4b-2c` from `engine/excalidraw` 747d41d.** The lead's queue after the wave-5 engine round: V1's fourth re-check, the security sweep's web items, the cut-over gap list. Each fix has a test that fails on 747d41d and passes with it (run against the base commit's sources in a detached worktree: 7 of the new tests fail there, the rest of the files pass).
  - **V1 medium (round 4, L7): the server's derived writes counted as other users' writes.** A push that moves a box makes the room re-snap the arrow bound to it (rule 7, `room/fit.ts`): a version bump with no op of this tab's, reported only as an `ack.derived` row. `Sent.ahead` listed only the pushes carrying the element and `recordAckedVersions` kept only `r.v`, so a recolour of the arrow lost behind two moves of its box read as "two writes since, ours landed and someone wrote after" and was dropped with the notice "Someone else changed an object you edited" when nobody had. Now `ahead` is every push in flight when the lost one was built, the acked versions include the derived rows, and the decision compares the server copy against the lost push's `ref` plus the fields the server rewrote for the pushes ahead (`fix` of own ops and derived rows: `effectiveRef`), so the hypothesis "ours landed, nobody wrote after" can hold when the arrow's geometry moved with its box. `w4b-fixes.test.ts` (L7 never-arrived: replayed, no notice; L7 landed with the ack lost: kept, no notice; L7c control), on the protocol fake's new scripted derived writes (`FakeRoom.derive`, a `path` patch and a `derived` row per moved target).
  - **The client half of 2A's doc-key CR (CONTRACTS_REV 9, W4B-1: `welcome.docClocks` / `snapshot.docClocks`).** `decideLostDoc` compares the key's clock with the lost push's base clock (kept per in-flight doc write, `LostDocRecord.clock`, in the offline queue too): at or below it nobody wrote the key since, so the write goes again; above it, the server's value stands unless it is the one we sent, and the person is told (`superseded` event with id `doc:<key>`; the board page says "Someone else changed a board setting you edited while you were reconnecting"). That closes V1 R9 (A's lost-but-landed red, B's blue back, A's reconnect: blue stays, 10/10 before it was overwritten). The field is read loosely (`docClockOf`), so the core builds with `protocol.ts` before or after rev 9; a room without it keeps the value-only rule. A chain of lost writes replays when a later write's base is past the key's clock. `w4b-fixes.test.ts` (R9 live and across a reload, never-landed with and without another writer, the pre-rev-9 fallback, the pure rule).
  - **The redacted-tombstone undo display:** already the case since wave 5 (`keepTombstoneContent`: a redacted `c` op for an element this tab holds keeps the content, only `TOMBSTONE_KEYS` are taken; the full `c` op of the undelete replaces it). No code change; a regression test for the deleting tab (`w4b-fixes.test.ts`: alice deletes, the ack is lost, the catch-up brings the tombstone redacted, her undo revives the element whole on every tab, hashes converge).

- **Excalidraw wave 4c, owner 2C (web engine), branch `engine/w4c-2c` from `engine/excalidraw` 9b65e89: the V1 verifier's round-6 medium (wave 4b's output).** The doc-key clock rule (`canvas/sync/core/engine.ts` `decideLostDocKey`, the consumer half of `EXCALIDRAW-CONTRACTS.md` W4B-1) lost the LATER of two OWN writes of one board-document key when the link broke between them, and the page said "Someone else changed a board setting you edited while you were reconnecting — their version is kept" with nobody else on the board (reachable from the session settings' `workshop` key, the start view and tag definitions on a slow link).
  - **Root cause:** two `setDoc` writes of one key within one round trip go out as two pushes built on the same base clock (the client clock moves only on `changes`/welcome/snapshot; `MAX_IN_FLIGHT_PUSHES` 4). The first lands (clock c1), its `changes` and ack arrive; the socket drops before the second reaches the room. At the welcome `docClocks[key]` = c1 is above the lost write's base, and the rule read any clock above the base as another user's write: the write was dropped as superseded, the local value went back, Saved showed. The element rule survives the same shape through `Sent.ahead` / `effectivePv`; the doc rule had no counterpart.
  - **Fix (`engine.ts`, `types.ts`):** per doc write in flight the core keeps the seqs of the pushes it was built behind (`inflightDocAhead`) and, per acked push that wrote doc keys, the room's `ack.clock` for them (`ackedDocClock`, pruned like `ackedVersion`; a key in `ack.docDenied` does not count). The real room's ack clock is the batch clock `merge.ts` writes to `lb_doc.clock`, so it equals `docClocks[key]` when nobody wrote after. A lost write's record (a socket's, or the offline queue's `lostDoc`, `lostDocRecordOf`) carries, per key, the clock of the own acked write ahead when it is above the push's base (`LostDocRecord.clocks`, additive and optional), and `decideLostDocKey` counts other writes from `max(clock, clocks[key])`: at or below it the write goes again; above it the server's value stands unless it is ours, and the person is told. Rooms before rev 9 and older queues decide as before. No wire change (`CONTRACTS_REV` stays 9); `EXCALIDRAW-CONTRACTS.md` W4C-2C-1 records the consumer rule; `W5-ENGINE.md` §5a's doc-key row has the round.
  - **Tests (fail on 9b65e89, pass now):** `apps/web/src/canvas/sync/core/w4c-fixes.test.ts` on the protocol fake, whose session gained `upLatencyMs` (a slow uplink alone, the verifier's proxy `delayUpMs`; 400 ms up, 50 ms down): DB1 live (blue stored, no event, Saved; before: `superseded doc:tagDef:topic`, red stored), DB1 across a reload (the queue's record `clock` 1, `clocks {key: 2}`, a second key with no own write ahead has none; before: no `clocks`), another writer after the own acked write (theirs stands and A is told: the lift stops at the own write), the DB1c control, the pure rule with per-key clocks. 3 of the 5 fail before; the two guards pass before and after. **The verifier's repro on the real room** (`Draftroom-spike-v1`'s untracked `apps/sync/test/v1-probes`, copied in for the run and removed, headless core on the real sync process, ephemeral ports): `v1r6-docburst` DB1 20/20 (acksMid 1 in every rep, stored blue, events [], saved; before, the verifier's 20/20 red + notice), DB1c 20/20; `v1r6-reload` DB1q 20/20 (the adopted record `clock 1, clocks {…: 2}`, stored blue, no notice). Logs: scratchpad `w4c/{docburst,reload,unit-gate}.log`, `w4c/probe-results.log`.

A plain wheel zooms 10% a notch around the pointer for Mouse, never for Trackpad, and under Auto for a mouse notch (lines/pages, or whole hundreds of px on one axis). **Behaviour change:** under the default Auto a mouse-wheel notch now zooms where it used to pan. W5-40 (CR-W5-3A-1): a connector's kind is its own next-style value (`StyleValues.arrowKind` → `currentItemArrowType`), so Straight after Elbow draws straight and a connector pick no longer rounds the next rectangle; the attach-time workaround in `parts/tools.ts` is gone. W5-52: `INITIAL_DATA` sets `currentItemRoundness: 'sharp'`, so drawn rectangles and diamonds have `roundness: null` like template and converted shapes (stroke width unchanged: the engine's medium already is the panel's M). Facade additions only: `LobbySettings` `wheel`, `StyleValues.arrowKind`, the `pinch` event's optional `touch`.

Selection clicks still record no step. `a11y-keyboard-safety.spec.ts` F11 `needs()` removed; the test now checks the redo too. CR-3D-4: `LobbyHistory.record` drops an index-only change that keeps the element's place in paint order (Excalidraw's `syncInvalidIndices` re-key) and folds the new key into the entries that hold that index; a new mind-map topic and its first words are one step (`HistoryMark.creation`, `joinAction`; the runtime always makes a joined write when a topic it just made closes its editor). `history-join.ts` delegates to `mergeTop`, so put-back fields no longer leave steps that undo nothing. Notes and the "Central topic" placeholder stay two steps (guarded by tests). The stored body is never rewritten, and an unreadable body or the tldraw sync still gets the records. The dialog says "Saved before the canvas update: shown converted to the new canvas." Only an owner changes, removes or makes an owner (`OWNER_ONLY`). The last owner stays (`LAST_OWNER`), and so does the personal workspace's person (`PERSONAL_WORKSPACE_OWNER`). Raising a role needs a creator caller, and admin/owner need a creator target. Removal revokes the member's board grants in that workspace in the same transaction (workspace row `FOR NO KEY UPDATE` first). Both actions are audited, and `accessChanged` reaches every board whose resolved role moved (capped at 500, most recently opened first; the periodic re-check covers the rest). No retries. **The one failure is a test race, recorded, not fixed:** `a11y-phone-overlays.spec.ts:89` R2-04 "Gather everyone shows a phone the leader view at a readable zoom" failed `expect(bv.zoom).toBeLessThan(0.45)` with 0.5. Its trace shows the follower's camera was still its own when the poll passed (zoom 0.5, centre (698, 426) against the leader's (1985, 1168) at 0.44). The toast was visible before the gathered camera arrived, and the poll's `> 0.43` accepted the phone's own zoom. Re-run alone on the merged tree it passed 3/3 and then 20/20. Wave B does not touch the test, the gather/follow path (`features/workshops`, `canvas/presence`) or the toast. It is not reproduced on e63586d, so "pre-existing" rests on that reasoning. Tightening the poll to wait for the leader's zoom is a follow-up. No failure needed a fix, so the integration adds no code commit. Listeners in 7240-7279 afterwards: 0.
  - **Protocol and deploy order.** **L7 changes the board WebSocket handshake.** The new web offers the subprotocols `lobby.board.v1` and `lobby.ticket.<ticket>` and puts no ticket in the URL; a ticket that is not an HTTP token stays in the URL. The new sync reads the subprotocol ticket first, then `?ticket=` (kept for at least one release), verifies it before the upgrade and answers exactly `Sec-WebSocket-Protocol: lobby.board.v1`. The constants live in `apps/sync/src/app.ts` and `apps/web/src/board/ticket-protocol.ts`, and a sync test checks they match. The message protocol is unchanged, `CONTRACTS_REV` stays 10, and `packages/canvas-schema/src/protocol.ts` was not edited (its header comment still describes `?ticket=`; changing it needs a CR under that file's rule). A cut-over sync (today's staging) knows only `?ticket=`, so the new web on the old sync would connect without a ticket and fail. **The sync must be live before the web.** Old tabs and cached bundles keep working on the new sync. Additive HTTP contracts: `CheckpointContent.converted` (B4, optional; older clients ignore it) and the workspace-member routes and types (B5; the new web's Share dialog needs the new api for PATCH/DELETE). No migration (the api stays at `0023`), no SQLite, storage, backup or converter change (`CONVERTER_VERSION` unchanged; v1 checkpoint bodies are converted on read and never rewritten). **Deploy order: api → sync → web** (api and sync are independent of each other; both must be live before the web). Right after the web deploy, open a board on staging and check in DevTools that the socket request carries `lobby.ticket.*` and the response says `lobby.board.v1`. Render's edge passing `Sec-WebSocket-Protocol` both ways is unverified, and the client has no fallback to `?ticket=`; if the edge strips it every board socket fails, and the fix is to roll back the web only (the new sync still takes `?ticket=`). With no migration, storage, backup or converter change, the rollback paths are unchanged and no item on this branch has to be held for rollback safety.
  - **Follow-ups (reviewers' minor findings and the workstreams' notes; none blocks the deploy).** (1) B1: `keepsPaintOrder` scans every live element per index-only change, so a mass re-key that keeps the order is quadratic (5,000 elements 686 ms, 20,000 elements 15.5 s on the main thread); judge them in one sorted pass. (2) B1: a restored selection drops `selectedGroupIds`, so a group selected as a group comes back as its members (not checked in a browser). A redo can restore an empty selection when the editor-close boundary ran before the engine applied a just-made select. (3) B2: the Auto wheel guess ignores `devicePixelRatio` and browser zoom, so a mouse under 125%/150% display scaling probably pans (reasoned, not measured); scale the guess. The real-iPad pinch check (one-finger lift after a Lobby-held pinch, iPad Safari pointer wiring) stays an owner item (P10). (4) B3: the phone sheet and the floating selection bar are not avoided by edge chips. (5) B4: before `?ticket=` is removed, move the headless client, the spike board session, the e2e forge sockets, `e2e/load` and the sync harness clients; consider a counter for old-transport connects. PARITY-5.5 converts synchronously on the api event loop (about 85 µs per record, so about 1.7 s for a 20k-record v1 board); add a per-snapshot cache or a size threshold if staging shows it. (6) B5: the PATCH refreshes an admin/owner target at the identity provider before confirming membership; check membership first. Offboarding is not complete: a leaver's invitations, links and pending requests survive, and an invite redeem racing a removal can leave a stale `board_members` row (`FOR SHARE` in `members.ts` `loadTarget`). The workspace lock was run only on PGlite; run `workspace-members.test.ts` once on a real Postgres. (7) Docs still describing these items as open (not edited here): `research/excalidraw/WAVE-3.md` (CR-3D-4, F11, CR 3b-10, CR-W5-3A-4, CR-W5-3A-1), `wave3/3D.md` deviations 1 and 4, SYNTHESIS LOOK-3, `EXCALIDRAW-CONTRACTS.md` (`settings.wheel`, `StyleValues.arrowKind`, the pinch `touch` field), `DEPLOY.md:330` and `DOMAIN-ACCESS-CONTRACTS.md:835` (leavers removed by hand), `M1-CONTRACTS.md` §5 (the two new routes). (8) `mindmap-structure.spec.ts`'s drop step still allows two undo presses; it passes with one. (9) Sync: when Excalidraw re-keys another person's element, the core pushes it as a local `z` change (the history now ignores it). (10) Process: git stashes are shared by every worktree. B2, B3 and B6 each popped another workstream's stash once during their before/after proofs and repaired it. The six branches touch disjoint files apart from the two one-line `session.ts` edits above, so no branch carries another's files. Do the before/after proofs in a scratch worktree, not with `git stash`. B2's commits carry a `Claude Sonnet 5.5` trailer from that session's attribution rule. (11) `a11y-phone-overlays.spec.ts` R2-04: make the first poll wait for the leader's zoom and centre, not just `> 0.43` (the race in the gate).

The api goes first for 0024's features (the pre-0024 web, and any tab opened before the web deploy, sends no `x-lobby-client`: it sees a selection vote as a board vote, its casts still follow the selection, and it gets no `access_granted` items).
