  - **Compat refix, round 1 (a review of follow-up 1: 1 major and 1 minor, one cause; verified on 6cd47db8 and fixed, with tests that failed first). Within `CONTRACTS_REV` 12, no wire, room, api, dependency, lockfile, migration or environment change; web engine core only.**
    1. **Major (and the minor, the same defect): a 437b4e7 page's offline queue, taken over by the rev-12 web, erased the planned minutes.** Verified: `OfflineEntry` and `LostPushRecord` were the same shape at 437b4e7 and 6cd47db8, so the rev-12 core read a rev-11 page's IndexedDB queue (`lobby-offline`) as its own, after a reload or as the next tab of the board (a closed tab's queue), and sent its `present: {index}` (437b4e7's Frames panel writes `present` whole and never knew `mins`) on a socket whose connect URL says `rev=12`. The room took `writerRev` from the socket (`s.reader` = 12), so `lobbyWriteOf` kept nothing and the frame was stored as `{index}`: the minutes gone for everyone, Saved, no fix, no STALE. Lost pushes the old page left (replayed after the receipts or the lost-push decision) the same. **Fix (the smallest; the wire and the room unchanged):** the web stamps what it queues with the revision of the page that built it (optional `OfflineEntry.rev` and `LostPushRecord.rev`, `canvas/sync/types.ts`; absent means `UNANNOUNCED_REV`, every queue 437b4e7 wrote). The engine core (`core/engine.ts`, `withOlderLobby`) completes an older page's queued copy with canvas-schema's `lobbyWriteOf` against the server copy before anything of it is pushed or compared: in `settleDirty`, the first time the element has a server copy (so behind a reset snapshot older than the element, at its catch-up), and a lost record's copy in `replayLost`. Each adopted copy is completed once; one that is decided with nothing to send (a lost push that landed) is forgotten (`dropOlderUntracked`), so a later change of this page's own (a rev-12 removal of the minutes) is never completed as if the old page had made it. A not yet completed older copy keeps its own revision when this page writes the queue again; a 437b4e7 tab ignores the field (its store validates nothing). EXCALIDRAW-CONTRACTS §0b rule 4 and L-3 say so.
    - **Tests, each failing before the fix:** `apps/web/src/canvas/sync/core/older-queue.test.ts` (6, the FakeRoom harness, the queue put in 437b4e7's form): an offline reorder keeps `{index: 2, mins: 7}`; an offline removal of `present` sends `{mins: 7}`; a push out at the close (a lost record) replays with the minutes; behind a reset snapshot older than the frame, completed at its catch-up (the case the first version of this fix missed, found by the real-server test); an older record that landed is completed once only, so this page's own later removal of the minutes stands (failed on the first version: the removal was undone and nothing was sent); and the control, this page's own rev-12 queue sent as written (failed before only on the stamp). `apps/sync/test/verifier-v1/rev11-queue-reload.test.ts` (the real sync and the real engine core, as v1-offline): a rev-11 socket's page goes offline (or its push never reaches the room), reorders, persists; the reloaded page on `rev=12` takes the queue over; stored `{index: 2, mins: 7}` (both failed before: `{index: 2}`). `e2e/excalidraw/l-compat-rev11.spec.ts` **L-compat 1b** with the real 437b4e7 web (rebuilt in the session scratchpad as the kit says, deleted afterwards): alice plans minutes, bob's 437b4e7 tab goes offline and moves Wrap up (its IndexedDB holds `c:present` with no `rev`), closes; his context then gets this tree's web, whose next tab of the board takes the queue over on a `rev=12` socket: every frame keeps its minutes, Saved, no reject, every tab's hash the server's, the queue empty. With the engine fix reverse-applied (patch-revert) it failed: stored `{index: 1}`.
    - **Checks (on the final tree of this round):** `pnpm lint` (biome, 2,386 files, 0 errors, the same 4 older warnings); `pnpm -r typecheck` (all packages, 0 errors). Units: `apps/web` 3,951 passed (its 4 `node --test` files fail to load under vitest, as always; they run under `node --test`: the build gates 17/17); `packages/canvas-schema` 165/165; `apps/sync` (`--maxWorkers=4`) 824 passed / 1 skipped / 2 failed, both `test/presence/presence-rank.test.ts` at vitest's 5 s timeout (server presence code this round does not touch; alone with `--testTimeout=60000` 9/9 pass; under a parallel full run once also 3 other timing tests timed out, each passed serially). e2e chromium, one worker: `l-compat-rev11` with the real 437b4e7 build 5/5, then 15/15 (`--repeat-each=3`, `E2E_PORT_BASE` 7720 and 7728); `sync-durability`, `board-reload`, `sign-out-purge`, `gaps-leave-guard`, `board-session-stable`, `l6-stale-pairs`, `l5-agenda` 20/20 (`E2E_PORT_BASE=7724`), each run on the final engine code. Not run: the whole e2e suite, real Postgres (no api change), anything against Render, Clerk, R2 or the NAS.
  - **Compat refix, round 2 (a review of round 1: 2 minors; both verified on ff70611e and fixed, with tests that failed first). Within `CONTRACTS_REV` 12, no wire, room, api, dependency, lockfile, migration or environment change; web only (the offline queue store and one line of the engine core).**
    1. **Minor: a rev-12 page's queued removal of a frame's minutes was undone when a 437b4e7 tab took that queue over (the reverse of round 1).** Verified at 437b4e7 (`git show`): its `offline-idb.ts` (identical to ff70611e's) adopts every free-lock queue of the board and user (`r.boardId === o.boardId && r.uid === o.uid && r.tab !== o.tab`) whatever the entries' `rev`, its core never reads `rev` and sends on a socket that names none, so the room completed the rev-12 `present {index}` as a rev-11 write and kept the minutes; offline, its `offlineEntries` saved the queue again without `rev`, and the next rev-12 page completed it the same way. Proven with the real 437b4e7 web (L-compat 1c below: its tab adopted and cleared the queue). **Fix (the smallest that keeps the old tabs working):** this web writes its records in its own form, `boardId` = `q2:<board>` and key `q2:<board>|<uid>|<tab>` (`queueBoard`), in the same database, store and version (a new version would make every open 437b4e7 tab's `indexedDB.open('lobby-offline', 1)` fail, and a new store would need one); 437b4e7's `adoptOthers` and `load` never match it. This web's `load` still takes the older form over (other closed tabs', and its own tab's from before the reload, which it then deletes; the core completes those as rev-11 queues, round 1) and `purge` covers both forms. CRIT-18 holds in both directions: a Sign out deletes by user (`purgeOfflineQueuesOf`), so a 437b4e7 tab's own Sign out deletes this web's records too. **Known deploy-window limit:** a 437b4e7 tab's `purge` on a FORBIDDEN or NOT_FOUND close does not reach this form (the revoked user's own unsent edits, which the room refuses when sent; a Sign out still deletes them); after a web rollback to 437b4e7 this form waits, unread, until the web rolls forward again.
    2. **Minor (a regression of round 1): an adopted 437b4e7 offline create's older-copy marker was never cleared.** Verified with the FakeRoom harness: `settleDirty` returns early for a create, and no `dropOlderUntracked` ran after an ack, so the `olderCopies` mark outlived the acked create and a later rev-12 change of this page's own was completed as the old page's (an offline removal of the minutes put back, nothing sent, Saved; across a reload the entry stamped rev 11). **Fix:** the create's ok ack drops the mark (`onAckMessage`: the create was written whole, nothing of the old page's is left to complete).
    - **Tests, each failing before its fix:** `apps/web/src/canvas/sync/adapter/offline-idb-purge.test.ts` (3, the memory IndexedDB, with 437b4e7's store frozen as `apps/web/src/canvas/sync/testing/rev11-437b4e7/offline-idb.ts`, header and import paths only changed): a queue this web left is not adopted by the 437b4e7 store, the next page of this web takes it over with its `rev`, and the 437b4e7 Sign out deletes it (before: the 437b4e7 store adopted it); this web takes the older form over, its own tab's included, and leaves none in it; `purge` covers both forms (the last two failed on the form). `apps/web/src/canvas/sync/core/older-queue.test.ts` (2 more): an older offline create, then a rev-12 removal of the minutes offline sent after the reconnect (before: nothing sent, room `{index: 3, mins: 5}`), and across a reload stamped 12 and sent (before: stamped 11). **L-compat 1c** in `e2e/excalidraw/l-compat-rev11.spec.ts` with the real 437b4e7 web (rebuilt in the session scratchpad as the kit says, deleted afterwards): bob's context holds a tab of this web, which goes offline and lowers Warm-up to No time planned, then closes (queued `c:present`, `rev:12`, form `q2`); a new 437b4e7 tab of his opens the board and takes nothing; his next tab of this web sends it: stored `{index: 1}`, Saved, no reject, every tab's hash the server's. With the adapter fix reverse-applied (patch-revert, the form check relaxed in a throwaway copy of the spec) it failed: the 437b4e7 tab had adopted and cleared the queue. The e2e queue readers (`l-compat-rev11`, `sync-durability`) match both forms.
    - **Checks (on the final tree of this round):** `pnpm lint` (biome, 2,387 files, 0 errors, the same 4 older warnings); `pnpm -r typecheck` (all 15 packages, 0 errors). Units: `apps/web` 3,956 passed (its 4 `node --test` files fail to load under vitest, as always; under `node --test` the build gates 17/17); `packages/canvas-schema` 165/165; `apps/sync` (`--maxWorkers=4`) 824 passed / 1 skipped / 2 failed, both `test/presence/presence-rank.test.ts` at vitest's 5 s timeout, as in round 1 (alone with `--testTimeout=60000` 9/9 pass; no sync code changed); `verifier-v1/rev11-queue-reload.test.ts` 2/2. e2e chromium, one worker: `l-compat-rev11` with the real 437b4e7 build 12/12 (6 tests, `--repeat-each=2`, `E2E_PORT_BASE=7720`); `sync-durability`, `board-reload`, `sign-out-purge`, `gaps-leave-guard`, `board-session-stable`, `l6-stale-pairs`, `l5-agenda` 20/20 (`E2E_PORT_BASE=7700`). Not run: the whole e2e suite, real Postgres (no api change), anything against Render, Clerk, R2 or the NAS.
  - **O1/O2 sign-off for `CONTRACTS_REV` 12 (2026-10-10, recorded by the lead acting as O1 (sync and protocol) and O2 (client), under the owner's full takeover of 2026-10-08).** It was given on this evidence:
    - the per-reader downgrade at the single send point, and the writer-side keep (`frameForReader`, `lobbyWriteOf`);
    - the frozen real-437b4e7 schema fixture and `reader-rule.test.ts`, which run every rev-12 addition through every frame kind;
    - `l-compat-rev11.spec.ts` against the REAL 437b4e7 web build (L-compat 1, 1b, 1c, 2 and 4 passed 3/3);
    - two compat attack rounds, which found offline-queue handovers in both directions and fixed them;
    - the final gate at `6d273f18`: units 7,938 passed; real PG 1,795 passed; whole e2e 822/822 normally; stale pairs (a) 117/117 and (b) as expected.
    Open at sign-off: `l-compat-rev11.spec.ts:752` (L-compat 3, the step "W4: Mermaid paste as a diagram") failed 2 times in about 5 runs. The prompt was not shown on the NEW web's second paste. This is not a compatibility or data issue. It is being investigated on `wave-l/mermaid-flake` for a web-only follow-up.
  - **Follow-up: the L-compat 3 flake (W4's second Mermaid paste showed no prompt; branch `wave-l/mermaid-flake` from `wave-l/integration` 6d273f18; commit 7b6c3095, test-only, pushed, NOT merged, nothing to deploy).** A test race, not a product bug. The failing run's trace (whole suite with `E2E_DIRECT_EVENTS=1`, web-7706) shows: round 0's Paste as diagram brought the diagram into view with Lobby's 320 ms camera animation; round 1 started about 135 ms later and set the camera through the harness (`__lobbyHarness.setViewport` -> `port.viewport.set`, which does not stop the tween); the tween's next frame took the camera back, focusBoard's click landed on the still-moving diagram and selected "Grade", and at Ctrl+V its selection bar lay under the pointer, where Excalidraw's `pasteFromClipboard` takes no paste (the element under the last pointer position must be its canvas), so Lobby's onPaste never ran. Each link shown on its own: a harness camera move during an animated one is undone within 100 ms; with the pointer on the selection bar Ctrl+V gives no prompt, on empty canvas it does. `l-compat-rev11.spec.ts` now waits for `cameraIdle` before every camera move (as `ai-kit.ts` `showBounds` does). New `e2e/excalidraw/l4-mermaid-twice.spec.ts`: two pastes in a row with the first diagram still selected, one of its shapes selected (+ handles and selection bar on screen), nothing selected, and the view moved away. **Checks:** `pnpm lint` (0 errors, the same 4 older warnings), `pnpm -r typecheck` (all packages, e2e included), vitest `features/ai/diagram`, `features/canvas-ux` and `features/flow` (39 files, 514 passed); `l4-mermaid-twice` x30: **120/120**; `l4-*`: 17/17; `l-compat-rev11` with a real 437b4e7 web (rebuilt in the session scratchpad, deleted afterwards) x3: **18/18**. The race did not reproduce on demand: the unfixed L-compat 3 passed 33 of 33 local runs (alone, beside other runs, and with the animation lengthened to 3 s), since it needs the click to select a shape whose bar then reaches the pointer. Not fixed here (follow-ups): the harness `setViewport` could stop the tween as the board's own `viewport.set` does; the selection bar covers the "below" + handle of a single selected shape.

- **Hotfix H1 (2026-10-10, web only; Wave M's M1-1 and M1-2 shipped early).** FTU-1 was live: on a phone, selecting two items inside a frame blanked the whole board (React #185, "Maximum update depth exceeded" in SelectionBar) until a reload. The paths that triggered it: the palette's "select all notes", Ask citations, AI find and Gather.
  - Root cause: the selection bar is absolutely placed, so its width shrank to the room right of its left edge. Near a canvas edge it folded into a column, and a 1 px ceil kept flipping its half width, so the layout effect (which had no deps) looped.
  - Fix:
    - `.cux-selbar` is `width: max-content`;
    - the layout effect has deps and a pure fold with at most 2 measures per selection key per 500 ms;
    - `placeSelectionBar` is pure and idempotent, with 8 px hysteresis;
    - `FeatureBoundary` and `FeatureGuard` wrap every feature-rendered component, logging one `feature_crash <id>` line, resetting on a selection or board change, and staying off after 3 crashes in a minute.
  - Tests:
    - e2e `m1-phone-frame.spec.ts`: 6 phone cases failed with #185 on 2548db89 and pass now, with tablet and desktop no-hang guards;
    - e2e `m1-boundary.spec.ts`: it fails with the guards reverted;
    - unit tests in `placement.test.ts` (+11) and `feature-boundary.test.ts` (6).
  - Gate at `4cfd398f`: lint and typecheck clean; units 7,956 passed; node build guards 17/17; whole e2e 828/837, with 1 failure, `import-v1-limits.spec.ts:114`, a pre-existing load-sensitive flake that passes 3/3 alone and on the baseline. The independent review found no issues.
